//-------------MAIN---------------
script_name 'MAIN'
Camera.DoFade(0, Fade.Out)
Game.SetMaxWantedLevel(2)
Clock.SetTimeOfDay(21, 0)
Streaming.RequestCollision(-535.64, -300.451)
Streaming.LoadScene(-535.64, -300.451, 12.29)
$PLAYER1 = Player.Create(0, 405.0, -905.0, 11.0712)
$scplayer = Player.GetChar($PLAYER1)
// Debug: debug.txt sets DebugWantedLevel, and 0 leaves the wanted level alone
Player.AlterWantedLevelNoDrop($PLAYER1, DebugWantedLevel)
Weather.ForceNow(WeatherType.Foggy)
wait 0
declare_mission_flag $ONMISSION
if
  not Char.IsDead($scplayer)
goto_if_false @MAIN_DeadBust
Char.Undress($scplayer, 'PLAYER')
Streaming.LoadAllModelsNow
Char.Dress($scplayer)
$ONMISSION = 0

// Initialize the game state
{$INCLUDE lib/init.txt}

:MAIN_Start
wait 250
if
    Player.IsPlaying($PLAYER1)
then
    Streaming.SetAreaVisible(Interior.MainMap)
    Player.SetControl($PLAYER1, False)
    Mission.LoadAndLaunchInternal(0)
    start_new_script @Star
    start_new_script @Save
    start_new_script @VPHotel
    start_new_script @PackageToggle
    terminate_this_script
end

:MAIN_DeadBust
wait 250
goto @MAIN_Start

goto_if_false @Star_159
Sphere.Remove($SidewalkSphere)

:End_10

    $Endgame == 1
then
    goto @End_95
else
    goto @End_35
end

:End_35
while $Endgame == 0
    wait 250
    if
        Player.IsPlaying($PLAYER1)
    then
        if
            Player.CanStartMission($PLAYER1)
        then
            if
                Player.LocateAnyMeans3D($PLAYER1, -535.6, -325.521, 13.56, 2.0, 2.0, 2.0, False)
            then
                Text.PrintBigString("The End Is Simply The Beginning...", 6000, TextStyle.BottomRight)
                Mission.LoadAndLaunchInternal(7)
                goto @End_10
            else
                break
            end
        else
            break
        end
    else
        break
    end
end

:End_94

:Fae
script_name 'Fae'

:Fae_666
wait 0
if
    $Fairy_Mission_Started == 1
then
    terminate_this_script
else
    goto @Fae_801
end

:Fae_801
while not Player.LocateAnyMeans3D($PLAYER1, 602.3546, -1700.3068, 6.2431, 5.0, 5.0, 5.0, False)
    wait 0
    if and
        Player.LocateAnyMeans3D($PLAYER1, 602.3546, -1700.3068, 6.2431, 5.0, 5.0, 5.0, False)
        Player.IsInModel($PLAYER1, #SPEEDER)
    then
        if
            $SpeederDelivered == 0
        then
            Text.PrintHelp('SPEED')
            wait 5000
            goto @Fae_1000
        end
        if
            $Tool_Mission_Passed == 0
        then
            Text.PrintHelpString("Come back once you have completed ~g~Born of Dirt.")
            wait 5000
            goto @Fae_1000
        else
            goto @Fae_911
        end
    end
end

:Fae_911
wait 0
if and
  Player.CanStartMission($PLAYER1)
  $ONMISSION == 0
  Player.IsInModel($PLAYER1, #SPEEDER)
  Player.LocateAnyMeans3D($PLAYER1, 602.3546, -1700.3068, 6.2431, 5.0, 5.0, 5.0, True)
  $SpeederDelivered == 1
  $Tool_Mission_Passed == 1
then
    Player.SetControl($PLAYER1, False)
    Text.PrintBigString("Three Days Later...", 6000, TextStyle.BottomRight)
    Camera.DoFade(1500, Fade.Out)
    wait 3000
    Clock.SetTimeOfDay(0, 0)
    Player.WarpFromCarToCoord($PLAYER1, -371.9346, -1739.0928, 11.6376)
    Player.SetHeading($PLAYER1, 159.50)
    Mission.LoadAndLaunchInternal(8)
    $Fairy_Mission_Started = 1
    terminate_this_script
end

// Spheres

:Sphere
script_name 'Sphere'

:Sphere_10
wait 0
if
    $SphereCreated == 0
then
    if or
        Player.LocateAnyMeans3D($PLAYER1, -37.0038, -940.981, 10.459, 40.0, 40.0, 15.0, False)
        Player.LocateAnyMeans3D($PLAYER1, 46.0097, -936.7421, 24.573, 40.0, 40.0, 15.0, False)
        Player.LocateAnyMeans3D($PLAYER1, 154.5795, -1168.3979, 17.7805, 40.0, 40.0, 15.0, False)
        Player.LocateAnyMeans3D($PLAYER1, 154.5795, -1168.3979, 31.3094, 40.0, 40.0, 15.0, False)
    then
        $CarparkSphere = Sphere.Create(-37.0038, -940.981, 10.459, 3.0)
        $CarparkRoofSphere = Sphere.Create(46.0097, -936.7421, 24.573, 3.0)
        $CarparkSphere2 = Sphere.Create(154.5795, -1168.3979, 17.7805, 3.0)
        $CarparkRoofSphere2 = Sphere.Create(154.5795, -1168.3979, 31.3094, 3.0)
        $SphereCreated = 1
        goto @Sphere_20
    else
        goto @Sphere_30
    end
else
    goto @Sphere_30
end

:Sphere_20
wait 0
if and
    not Player.LocateAnyMeans3D($PLAYER1, -37.0038, -940.981, 10.459, 40.0, 40.0, 15.0, False)
    not Player.LocateAnyMeans3D($PLAYER1, 46.0097, -936.7421, 24.573, 40.0, 40.0, 15.0, False)
    not Player.LocateAnyMeans3D($PLAYER1, 154.5795, -1168.3979, 17.7805, 40.0, 40.0, 15.0, False)
    not Player.LocateAnyMeans3D($PLAYER1, 154.5795, -1168.3979, 31.3094, 40.0, 40.0, 15.0, False)
then
    Sphere.Remove($CarparkSphere)
    Sphere.Remove($CarparkSphere2)
    Sphere.Remove($CarparkRoofSphere)
    Sphere.Remove($CarparkRoofSphere2)
    $SphereCreated = 0
    goto @Sphere_30
else
    goto @Sphere_20
end

:Sphere_30

    Player.LocateAnyMeans3D($PLAYER1, 531.8765, -118.7697, 13.337, 40.0, 40.0, 15.0, False)

    goto @Sphere_40

    goto @Sphere_50
end

$TIMER = 120000
Sphere.Remove($GodsBathSphere)

Clock.SetTimeOfDay(6, 00)
Weather.Force(WeatherType.Rainy)

Camera.DoFade(500, Fade.In)
wait 500
Text.PrintHelpString("It looks like Poseidon isn't too keen on letting you into his family's penthouse.")

Text.PrintHelpString("I'd be on the lookout for golf-ball sized hail or a biblical plague soon.")
wait 5000
Text.PrintHelpString("Maybe next time, you'll think twice before tresspassing, eh?")
Hud.DisplayTimer($TIMER, TimerDirection.Down)

:Barrier
script_name 'Barrier'

:Barrier_10
wait 0
if
    Player.IsPlaying($PLAYER1)
then
    if
        $HomeSafe == 1
    then
        if
            $Tool_Mission_Passed == 1
        then
            if
                $Limey_Passed == 1
            then
                if
                    $Boss_Killed == 1
                then
                    goto @Barrier_900
                else
                    goto @Barrier_600
                end
            else
                goto @Barrier_470
            end
        else
            goto @Barrier_300
        end
    else
        goto @Barrier_15
    end
else
    goto @Barrier_1000
end

// ============================================================================
// STAGE 1: Early Game / Lockdown ($HomeSafe == 0)  0.2 Density
// ============================================================================
:Barrier_15
wait 0
if
    $HomeSafe == 0
then
    World.SetCarDensityMultiplier(0.2)
    World.SetPedDensityMultiplier(0.2)

    if or
        Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'DTOWN')
        Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'HAVANA')
        Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'HAITI')
        Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'PORNI')
        Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'A_PORT')
        Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'GOLFC')
    then
        $PlayerOutside = 1
        goto @Barrier_16
    else
        goto @Barrier_200
    end
else
    goto @Barrier_10
end

:Barrier_16
Text.PrintHelpString("Leave this area at once!")
$PlayerHealth = Player.GetHealth($PLAYER1)
if
    $PlayerHealth > 20
then
    $PlayerHealth -= 10
else
    $PlayerHealth -= 1
end
Player.SetHealth($PLAYER1, $PlayerHealth)
wait 1000
if
    $PlayerHealth < 5
then
    goto @Barrier_500
else
    goto @Barrier_10
end

:Barrier_200
wait 0
if or
    Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'BEACH1')
    Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'BEACH2')
    Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'BEACH3')
    Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'STARI')
then
    $PlayerOutside = 0
    Player.SetHealth($PLAYER1, 100)
    goto @Barrier_10
else
    goto @Barrier_15
end

// ============================================================================
// STAGE 2: Post-Mission 2 ($HomeSafe == 1)  0.4 Density
// ============================================================================
:Barrier_300
wait 0
World.SetCarDensityMultiplier(0.4)
World.SetPedDensityMultiplier(0.4)

if or
    Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'BEACH1')
    Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'BEACH2')
    Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'BEACH3')
    Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'STARI')
    Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'HAVANA')
    Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'DOCKS')
then
    Player.SetHealth($PLAYER1, 100)
    $PlayerOutside = 0
    goto @Barrier_10
else
    goto @Barrier_400
end

:Barrier_400
wait 2000
if or
    Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'DTOWN')
    Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'HAITI')
    Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'PORNI')
    Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'GOLFC')
    Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'A_PORT')
then
    Text.PrintHelpString("Leave this area at once!")
    $PlayerOutside = 1
    goto @Barrier_450
else
    goto @Barrier_300
end

:Barrier_450
wait 1000
$PlayerHealth = Player.GetHealth($PLAYER1)
if
    $PlayerHealth > 20
then
    $PlayerHealth -= 10
else
    $PlayerHealth -= 1
end
Player.SetHealth($PLAYER1, $PlayerHealth)
if
    $PlayerHealth < 5
then
    goto @Barrier_500
else
    goto @Barrier_400
end

// ============================================================================
// STAGE 3: Intermission / Pre-Mission 3 ($Intermission_Passed == 1)  0.6 Density
// ============================================================================
:Barrier_470
wait 0
if
    $Limey_Passed == 0
then
    World.SetCarDensityMultiplier(0.6)
    World.SetPedDensityMultiplier(0.6)

    if or
        Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'BEACH1')
        Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'BEACH2')
        Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'BEACH3')
        Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'STARI')
        Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'HAVANA')
        Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'DOCKS')
    then
        Player.SetHealth($PLAYER1, 100)
        $PlayerOutside = 0
        goto @Barrier_10
    else
        goto @Barrier_400
    end
else
    goto @Barrier_10
end

// ============================================================================
// PUNISHMENT / WARP SEQUENCE
// ============================================================================
:Barrier_500
Player.SetControl($PLAYER1, False)
Camera.DoFade(500, Fade.Out)
wait 1000
if
    Player.IsInAnyCar($PLAYER1)
then
    goto @Barrier_550
else
    goto @Barrier_560
end

:Barrier_550
Weather.Force(WeatherType.Foggy)
Player.WarpFromCarToCoord($PLAYER1, 422.55, 85.14, 24.00)
Camera.DoFade(1000, Fade.In)
wait 1000
Text.PrintHelpString("Do not set foot on our lands again!")
wait 1000
Player.SetControl($PLAYER1, True)
wait 250
Player.ExplodeHead($PLAYER1)
goto @Barrier_10

:Barrier_560
Weather.Force(WeatherType.Foggy)
Player.SetCoordinates($PLAYER1, 422.55, 85.14, 24.00)
wait 1000
Camera.DoFade(1000, Fade.In)
wait 1000
Text.PrintHelpString("Do not set foot on our lands again!")
Player.SetControl($PLAYER1, True)
wait 500
Player.ExplodeHead($PLAYER1)
goto @Barrier_10

// ============================================================================
// STAGE 4: Post-Mission 3 ($Limey_Passed == 1)  0.8 Density
// ============================================================================
:Barrier_600
wait 3000
if
    $Boss_Killed == 0
then
    World.SetCarDensityMultiplier(2.0)
    World.SetPedDensityMultiplier(2.0)

    if or
        Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'HAITI')
        Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'HAVANA')
        Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'GOLFC')
    then
        Weather.Release()
        $PlayerOutside = 0
        goto @Barrier_10
    else
        goto @Barrier_700
    end
else
    goto @Barrier_10
end

:Barrier_700
wait 1000
if or
    Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'BEACH1')
    Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'BEACH2')
    Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'BEACH3')
    Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'STARI')
then
    $PlayerOutside = 0
    goto @Barrier_10
else
    goto @Barrier_750
end

:Barrier_750
if or
    Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'DTOWN')
    Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'PORNI')
    Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'A_PORT')
then
    Weather.Force(WeatherType.Foggy)
    Text.PrintHelpString("Leave this area at once!")
    $PlayerOutside = 1
    goto @Barrier_800
else
    goto @Barrier_600
end

:Barrier_800
wait 4000
if or
    Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'DTOWN')
    Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'PORNI')
    Player.IsInZone($PLAYER1, 'A_PORT')
then
    $PlayerHealth = Player.GetHealth($PLAYER1)
    if
        $PlayerHealth > 20
    then
        $PlayerHealth -= 10
    else
        $PlayerHealth -= 1
    end
else
    goto @Barrier_600
end
Player.SetHealth($PLAYER1, $PlayerHealth)
wait 1000
if
    $PlayerHealth < 5
then
    goto @Barrier_500
else
    goto @Barrier_700
end

// ============================================================================
// STAGE 5: Story Completed ($Boss_Killed == 1)  1.0 Density Restored
// ============================================================================
:Barrier_900
wait 3000
World.SetCarDensityMultiplier(4.0)
World.SetPedDensityMultiplier(4.0)
Weather.Release()
$PlayerOutside = 0
goto @Barrier_10

// ============================================================================
// SCRIPT RESTART / CLEANUP
// ============================================================================
:Barrier_1000
wait 0
if
    not Player.IsPlaying($PLAYER1)
then
    wait 5000
    start_new_script @Barrier
    terminate_this_script
else
    goto @Barrier_10
end

:Barrier_6666
terminate_this_script

    $ONMISSION == 0

        $HomeSafe == 1

        goto @Cell_02
    else
        goto @Cell_1995
    end
else
    goto @Cell_01
end

    Audio.HasMissionAudioLoaded(MissionAudioSlot.Slot1)
then
    Audio.PlayMissionAudio(MissionAudioSlot.Slot1)
    Text.PrintHelpForever('ANSWER')
    goto @Cell_03
else
    goto @Cell_1990
end

:Cell_03
wait 0

    Pad.IsKeyPressed(KeyCode.Tab)
then
    Player.SetControl($PLAYER1, False)
    Char.SetAnsweringMobile($scplayer, True)
    Text.ClearHelp()
    Audio.ClearMissionAudio(1)
    goto @Cell_04
else
    goto @Cell_1998
end

:Cell_04
wait 1000
play_audio_stream_1channel "Audio/SSA/payphone.mp3", false, 0.5

wait 3000
Char.SetAnsweringMobile($scplayer, False)
Player.SetControl($PLAYER1, True)

start_new_script @Pier

:Mall_496

:PackageToggle
script_name 'PakTog'
$FirstPackageTold = 0                 // Flag to ensure tutorial only triggers once

:PakTog_Loop
while Player.IsPlaying($PLAYER1)
    wait 0
    $Packages = Game.GetCollectablesCollected()

    // Check if player just collected their first package to trigger full tutorial
    if and
        $Packages >= 1
        $FirstPackageTold == 0
    then
        $FirstPackageTold = 1

        Text.PrintHelp('PACKHL1')
        wait 5000

        Text.PrintHelp('PACKHL2')
        wait 5000

        Text.PrintHelp('PACKHLP')
    end

    // ============================================================================
    // MILESTONE REWARDS LOGIC (Runs continuously regardless of tracker ON/OFF)
    // ============================================================================
    if $Packages >= 9
    then
        if 4@ == 0
        then
            4@ = 1
            Stat.PlayerMadeProgress(10)
            Text.PrintHelp('PICK1')
            $StarfishArmor = Pickup.Create(#BODYARMOUR, PickupType.OnStreet, -536.524, -304.216, 11.4)
        end
    end

    if $Packages >= 18
    then
        if 5@ == 0
        then
            5@ = 1
            Stat.PlayerMadeProgress(10)
            Text.PrintHelp('PICK2')
            $StarfishBlade = Pickup.Create(#KNIFECUR, PickupType.OnStreet, -499.192, -245.979, 10.4)
        end
    end

    if $Packages >= 36
    then
        if 6@ == 0
        then
            6@ = 1
            Stat.PlayerMadeProgress(10)
            Text.PrintHelp('PICK3')
            $StarfishPython = Pickup.CreateWithAmmo(#PYTHON, PickupType.OnStreet, 300, -508.021, -245.979, 10.4)
        end
    end

    if $Packages >= 54
    then
        if 7@ == 0
        then
            7@ = 1
            Stat.PlayerMadeProgress(10)
            Text.PrintHelp('PICK4')
            $StarfishSniper = Pickup.CreateWithAmmo(#SNIPER, PickupType.OnStreet, 40, -510.175, -245.979, 10.4)
        end
    end

    if $Packages >= 72
    then
        if 8@ == 0
        then
            8@ = 1
            Stat.PlayerMadeProgress(10)
            Text.PrintHelp('PICK5')
            $StarfishMinigun = Pickup.CreateWithAmmo(#MINIGUN, PickupType.OnStreet, 5000, -515.431, -245.979, 10.4)
            CarGenerator.Switch($Squalo, 101)
        end
    end

    if $Packages >= 88
    then
        if 9@ == 0
        then
            9@ = 1
            Text.PrintHelp('PACKBON')
            Player.AddScore($PLAYER1, 50000)
            play_audio_stream_1channel "Audio/SSA/miscom.wav", false, 0.5
            Stat.PlayerMadeProgress(10)
        end
    end

    // --- TRACKER TOGGLE CONTROLS ---
    if
        not $Packages >= 88
    then
        // Press 'O' to turn OFF radar tracker
        if
            Pad.IsKeyPressed(KeyCode.O)
        then
            Text.PrintHelp('PACKOFF')
            $PackHunt = 0
            Blip.Remove($CurrentPackBlip)
            terminate_all_scripts_with_this_name 'PACKAGE'
            wait 500
        else
        // Press 'P' to turn ON radar tracker
            if Pad.IsKeyPressed(KeyCode.P)
            then
                $PackHunt = 1
                Text.PrintHelp('PACKON')
                wait 0
                start_new_script @PACKAGE
                wait 500
            end
        end
    else
        // Player pressed toggle after collecting everything
        if or
            Pad.IsKeyPressed(KeyCode.O)
            Pad.IsKeyPressed(KeyCode.P)
        then
            Text.PrintHelp('PACKDON')
        end
    end
end

wait 250
goto @PakTog_Loop


// ============================================================================
// RADAR GUIDE THREAD (Handles blips and coordinates when active)
// ============================================================================
:PACKAGE
script_name 'PACKAGE'

0@ = 0                                 // Active Target Package Index (0 to 87)
1@ = 1                                 // Blip Redraw Flag (1 = Needs New Blip)

:PACKAGE_Loop
while Player.IsPlaying($PLAYER1)
    wait 250

    // --- RADAR TRACKING LOGIC ---
    if 0@ <= 87
    then
        gosub @Get_Package_Coords

        // Draw/Redraw blip on active target package
        if 1@ == 1
        then
            1@ = 0
            Blip.Remove($CurrentPackBlip)
            $CurrentPackBlip = Blip.AddForCoord($PackCoordX, $PackCoordY, $PackCoordZ)
            Blip.ChangeColor($CurrentPackBlip, BlipColor.Red)
        end

        // PROXIMITY CHECK: Only advance 0@ IF player actually collects THIS target package
        if Player.LocateAnyMeans3D($PLAYER1, $PackCoordX, $PackCoordY, $PackCoordZ, 3.5, 3.5, 3.5, False)
        then
            0@ += 1                  // Target collected! Advance to next coordinate index
            1@ = 1                   // Signal blip redraw for the next package
            wait 1000                // Debounce pause so index doesn't double-increment
        end
    else
        // All package spots cleared
        Blip.Remove($CurrentPackBlip)
        terminate_this_script
    end
end

wait 250
goto @PACKAGE_Loop

if
    Pickup.HasBeenCollected($PhoneHelp)
then
    goto @Help_71
else
    if
        Pickup.HasBeenCollected($PhoneHelp)
    then
        goto @Help_400
    else
        if
            Pickup.HasBeenCollected($BUSTED_HELP1)
        then
            goto @Help_400
        else
            if
                Pickup.HasBeenCollected($WASTED_HELP1)
            then
                goto @Help_682
            else
                if
                    Pickup.HasBeenCollected($BeachJettyInfo)
                then
                    goto @Help_777
                else
                    if or
                        Pickup.HasBeenCollected($BeachBribe1)
                        Pickup.HasBeenCollected($HavanaBribe)
                        Pickup.HasBeenCollected($BeachBribe2)
                        Pickup.HasBeenCollected($StarfishBribe)
                        Pickup.HasBeenCollected($ViceportBribe)
                        Pickup.HasBeenCollected($VicePointBribe)
                    then
                        goto @Help_3184
                    else
                        if
                            Pickup.HasBeenCollected($MarinaHelp)
                        then
                            goto @Help_3698
                        else
                            if
                                Pickup.HasBeenCollected($BarrierHelp)
                            then
                                goto @Help_4210
                            else
                                if
                                    $Barrier1 == 1
                                then
                                    goto @Help_5210
                                else
                                    if
                                        $Barrier2 == 1
                                    then
                                        goto @Help_5420
                                    else
                                        if
                                            Player.IsOnAnyBike($PLAYER1)
                                        then
                                            goto @Help_5600
                                        else
                                            if
                                                $PRINT_CAR_HELP == 0
                                            then
                                                goto @Help_5800
                                            else
                                                if and
                                                    $Barrier1Done == 1
                                                    $Barrier2Done == 1
                                                then
                                                    $HelpDone == 1
                                                    goto @Help_6000
                                                else
                                                    goto @Help_10
                                                end
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end

  $ONMISSION == 0

  $SaveCreated == 0

  Pickup.HasBeenCollected($StarfishTape)

:JewelryRobbery
script_name 'Jewelry'

:Jewelry_Init
wait 0
Streaming.LoadSpecialCharacter(16, 'S_KEEP')
Streaming.RequestModel(#SPECIAL16)

:Jewelry_LoadModel
wait 0
if
    Streaming.HasSpecialCharacterLoaded(16)
then
    $JewelryClerk = Char.Create(PedType.CivMale, #SPECIAL16, 379.6753, 210.1892, 11.3862)
    Char.SetHeading($JewelryClerk, 268.67)
    Char.SetHealth($JewelryClerk, 100)
    $AimStartTime = 0
    $RobberyMoney = 0
    goto @Jewelry_MainLoop
else
    goto @Jewelry_LoadModel
end

:Jewelry_MainLoop
wait 0

if
    Char.IsDead($JewelryClerk)
then
    goto @Jewelry_TriggerAlarm
end

if
    Player.LocateAnyMeans3D($PLAYER1, 379.6753, 210.1892, 11.3862, 15.0, 15.0, 5.0, False)
then
    if
        Player.IsTargetingChar($PLAYER1, $JewelryClerk)
    then
        goto @Jewelry_AimingAtClerk
    else
        $AimStartTime = 0
        $RobberyMoney = 0
    end
else
    $AimStartTime = 0
    $RobberyMoney = 0
end

goto @Jewelry_MainLoop

:Jewelry_AimingAtClerk
wait 0
// Capture starting game time on first frame of aiming
if
    $AimStartTime == 0
then
    Clock.GetGameTimer($AimStartTime)
    Char.SetCrouch($JewelryClerk, True, 9999999)
end

Clock.GetGameTimer($CurrentTime)
set_var_int_to_var_int $ElapsedTime = $CurrentTime
sub_int_var_from_int_var $ElapsedTime -= $AimStartTime

// Award compounding score every 1000ms (1 second) of aiming
if
    $ElapsedTime >= 1000
then
    $RobberyMoney += 50
    Player.AddScore($PLAYER1, 50)
    Text.PrintBigString("+$50", 1000, TextStyle.MiddleSmaller)
    Sound.AddOneOffSound(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, ScriptSound.SoundPartMissionComplete)
    // Reset timer anchor for the next interval
    Clock.GetGameTimer($AimStartTime)
end

// Trigger alarm if player holds aim for 10+ seconds OR stops aiming after starting
if or
    $RobberyMoney >= 500
    not Player.IsTargetingChar($PLAYER1, $JewelryClerk)
then
    goto @Jewelry_TriggerAlarm
else
    goto @Jewelry_AimingAtClerk
end

:Jewelry_TriggerAlarm
Text.PrintHelpString("He called the cops! Lose the heat!")
Player.AlterWantedLevel($PLAYER1, 2)

if
    $AlarmOn == 0
then
    $JewelryAlarm = Sound.AddContinuous(379.6753, 210.1892, 11.3862, ScriptSound.SoundBankAlarmLoop)
    $AlarmOn = 1
end

if
    not Char.IsDead($JewelryClerk)
then
    Char.SetCrouch($JewelryClerk, True, 9999999)
end

:Jewelry_AlarmLoop
wait 0
if or
    not Player.IsWantedLevelGreater($PLAYER1, 0)
    Hud.AreWantedStarsFlashing()
then
    Sound.Remove($JewelryAlarm)
    $AlarmOn = 0
    $AimStartTime = 0
    $RobberyMoney = 0
    goto @Jewelry_ResetWait
else
    goto @Jewelry_AlarmLoop
end

:Jewelry_ResetWait
wait 1000
if
    not Player.LocateAnyMeans3D($PLAYER1, 379.6753, 210.1892, 11.3862, 30.0, 30.0, 10.0, False)
then
    Char.Delete($JewelryClerk)
    $JewelryClerk = Char.Create(PedType.CivMale, #SPECIAL16, 379.6753, 210.1892, 11.3862)
    Char.SetHeading($JewelryClerk, 268.67)
    Char.SetHealth($JewelryClerk, 100)
    goto @Jewelry_MainLoop
else
    goto @Jewelry_ResetWait
end

:VehHeal
script_name 'VehHeal'

:VehHeal_101
while not Player.IsInAnyCar($PLAYER1)
    wait 0
    if
        Player.CanStartMission($PLAYER1)
    then
        if
            Player.IsInAnyCar($PLAYER1)
        then
            $MyCar = Player.StoreCarIsInNoSave($PLAYER1)
            goto @VehHeal_102
        end
    end
end

:VehHeal_102
while Player.IsPlaying($PLAYER1)
    wait 0
    Car.GetHealth($MyCar, $CarHealth)
    $CarHealthBar = $CarHealth
    $CarHealthBar *= 100
    $CarHealthBar /= 1000
    Hud.DisplayCounterWithString($CarHealthBar, CounterDisplay.Bar, 'VEHHEAL')

    if
        not Player.IsPlaying($PLAYER1)
    then
        Car.Delete($MyCar)
        Hud.ClearCounter($CarHealthBar)
        start_new_script @VehHeal
        terminate_this_script
    end

    if
        not Player.IsInCar($PLAYER1, $MyCar)
    then
        Hud.ClearCounter($CarHealthBar)
        goto @VehHeal_101
    end
end

:Water
script_name 'Water'
$WarningActive = 0

:Water_10
while Player.IsPlaying($PLAYER1)
    $InWater = 0
    $WaterCar = 0
    wait 0
    if
        Char.IsInWater($scplayer)
    then
        $InWater = 1
    else
        if
            Player.IsInAnyCar($PLAYER1)
        then
            Player.StoreCarIsInNoSave($PLAYER1, $Any_Car)
            if or
                Car.IsInWater($Any_Car)
                Car.IsDead($Any_Car)
            then
                $WaterCar = 1
            end
        else
            Player.GetCoordinates($PLAYER1, $PlayerX, $PlayerY, $PlayerZ)
            Path.GetClosestCharNode($PlayerX, $PlayerY, $PlayerZ, $SafeX, $SafeY, $SafeZ)
        end
    end

    if or
        $WaterCar == 1
        $InWater == 1
    then
        Text.PrintHelpForever('TPORT2')
        $WarningActive = 1
        if
            Pad.IsKeyPressed(KeyCode.N)
        then
            Text.ClearHelp()
            $WarningActive = 0
            if
                $WaterCar == 1
            then
                Camera.DoFade(500, Fade.Out)
                wait 500
                Player.SetControl($PLAYER1, False)
                Player.WarpFromCarToCoord($PLAYER1, $SafeX, $SafeY, $SafeZ)
                wait 500
                Camera.DoFade(500, Fade.In)
                wait 500
                Player.SetControl($PLAYER1, True)
                break
            else
                Camera.DoFade(500, Fade.Out)
                wait 500
                Player.SetControl($PLAYER1, False)
                Player.SetCoordinates($PLAYER1, $SafeX ,$SafeY, $SafeZ)
                wait 500
                Camera.DoFade(500, Fade.In)
                wait 500
                Player.SetControl($PLAYER1, True)
                break
            end
        end
    else
        if
            $WarningActive == 1
        then
            Text.ClearHelp()
            $WarningActive = 0
        end
    end

    if
        $Limey_Passed == 1
    then
        if
            $Barrier2Done == 1
        then
            Text.PrintHelpString("Aquatic teleportation network disabled. Please be wary of ~g~sharks ~w~and other ~g~large fish.")
            wait 5000
            Text.PrintHelpString("You can no longer survive in large bodies of water.")
            terminate_this_script
        end
    end
end

:Water_20
wait 0
if
    $WarningActive == 1
then
    Text.ClearHelp()
    $WarningActive = 0
end
goto @Water_10

end

:BossDie_312
wait 3000
if or
    not Pad.IsKeyPressed(KeyCode.LeftShift)
    not Pad.IsButtonPressed(PadId.Pad1, Button.Cross)
then
    Camera.PointAtChar($Boss, CameraMode.Fixed, SwitchType.JumpCut)
    Char.ShutUp($Boss, true)
    Text.PrintString("You know damn well what!", 3500)
    Char.SetObjAimGunAtChar($Boss, $scplayer)
    goto @BossDie_313
else
    goto @BossDie_320
end

:BossDie_313
wait 3500
if or
    not Pad.IsKeyPressed(KeyCode.LeftShift)
    not Pad.IsButtonPressed(PadId.Pad1, Button.Cross)
then
    Text.PrintString("I know what you did with our client's daughter!", 5000)
    goto @BossDie_314
else
    goto @BossDie_320
end

:BossDie_314
wait 5000
if or
    not Pad.IsKeyPressed(KeyCode.LeftShift)
    not Pad.IsButtonPressed(PadId.Pad1, Button.Cross)
then
    Camera.PointAtPlayer($PLAYER1, CameraMode.Fixed, SwitchType.JumpCut)
    Text.PrintString("Boss, it wasn't like that! I swear!", 5000)
    goto @BossDie_315
else
    goto @BossDie_320
end

:BossDie_315
wait 5000
if or
    not Pad.IsKeyPressed(KeyCode.LeftShift)
    not Pad.IsButtonPressed(PadId.Pad1, Button.Cross)
then
    Camera.PointAtChar($Boss, CameraMode.Fixed, SwitchType.JumpCut)
    Text.PrintString("Save your excuses for someone who gives a shit!", 5000)
    wait 4000
    goto @BossDie_320
else
    goto @BossDie_320
end

:BossDie_320
Camera.DoFade(1500, Fade.Out)
wait 1500
Player.SetCoordinates($PLAYER1, -60.212, 86.2, 16.84)
wait 500
Camera.RestoreJumpcut
$TIMER = 60000
Camera.DoFade(1500, Fade.In)
wait 1500
Player.SetControl($PLAYER1, True)
Char.SetObjKillPlayerAnyMeans($Boss, $PLAYER1)
Text.PrintStringNow("Kill your boss!", 5000)
Hud.DisplayTimer($TIMER, TimerDirection.Down)

:BossDie_917
while not Char.IsDead($Boss)
wait 0
    if
        Char.IsDead($Boss)
    then
        Text.PrintStringNow("Go home.", 5000)
        $HomeBlip = Blip.AddForCoord(-530.692, -315.928, 10.62)
        $SidewalkSphere = Sphere.Create(-530.692, -315.928, 10.62, 3.0)
        break
    end

    if
        $TIMER == 0
    then
        goto @BossDie_1312
    end
end

:BossDie_1137
while not Player.LocateAnyMeans3D($PLAYER1, -530.692, -315.928, 10.62, 0.5, 0.5, 0.5, False)
    wait 0
    if
        Player.LocateAnyMeans3D($PLAYER1, -530.692, -315.928, 10.62, 0.5, 0.5, 0.5, False)
    then
        Player.SetControl($PLAYER1, False)
        Blip.Remove($HomeBlip)
        Sphere.Remove($SidewalkSphere)
        Camera.DoFade(1500, Fade.Out)
        wait 1500
        Player.WarpFromCarToCoord($PLAYER1, -535.6, -325.521, 13.56)
        goto @BossDie_1600
    end
end

:BossDie_1312
Hud.ClearTimer($TIMER)
$TIMER = 15000
Text.PrintBigString("Gods, you're the worst!", 3000, TextStyle.MiddleSmaller)
Player.SetHealth($PLAYER1, 35)
Player.AddArmour($PLAYER1, -99)
Player.RemoveAllWeapons($PLAYER1)
wait 500
Player.GiveWeapon($PLAYER1, WeaponType.SilencedIngram, 50)
World.ClearAreaOfCars(-50.123, 0.0, 12.0, 150.598, 100.235, 25.0)
Char.SetHealth($Boss, 500)
Text.PrintHelpString("Good luck, sucker!")
wait 500
Hud.DisplayTimer($TIMER, TimerDirection.Down)

:BossDie_1313
while not Char.IsDead($Boss)
    wait 0
    if
        $TIMER == 0
    then
        Char.RemoveElegantly($Boss)
        break
    end

    if
        Char.IsDead($Boss)
    then
        goto @BossDie_1600
    end
end

:BossDie_1400
Game.SetMaxWantedLevel(3)
Text.PrintBig('MISFAIL', 2000, TextStyle.Middle)
$ONMISSION = 0
Hud.ClearTimer($TIMER)
Blip.Remove($JettyBlip)
Phone.TurnOn($BossPhone)
terminate_this_script
return

:BossDie_1600
Hud.ClearTimer($TIMER)
Audio.LoadEndOfGameTune
Char.MarkAsNoLongerNeeded($Boss)
$Boss_Killed = 1
Camera.DoFade(1500, Fade.In)
wait 1500
Player.AddScore($PLAYER1, 100000)
Text.PrintWithNumberBig('MISPASS', 100000, 2000, TextStyle.Middle)
play_audio_stream_1channel "Audio/SSA/miscom.wav", false, 0.5
Restart.AddHospital(-822.6161, 1149.8101, 12.4111, 2.57)
Stat.RegisterMissionPassed('BURY')
Stat.PlayerMadeProgress(15)
$ONMISSION = 0
Audio.LoadEndOfGameTune
wait 500
start_new_script @End
terminate_this_script
return

//-------------Mission 6---------------

:Endgame
script_name 'Endgame'

:Endgame_LoadModels
wait 250
Streaming.RequestModel(#SABRE)
Streaming.RequestModel(#WFYG1)

:Endgame_WaitModels
wait 0
if and
    Streaming.HasModelLoaded(#SABRE)
    Streaming.HasModelLoaded(#WFYG1)
then
    Player.SetControl($PLAYER1, False)
    $Sabre = Car.Create(#SABRE, -535.8, -323.328, 13.62)
    wait 250
    $EndOfGamePed = Char.Create(PedType.CivFemale, #WFYG1, -530.692, -315.928, 13.62)
    wait 250
else
    goto @Endgame_WaitModels
end

Camera.PointAtChar($EndOfGamePed, CameraMode.FollowPed, SwitchType.JumpCut)
Camera.DoFade(1500, Fade.In)
wait 1500

:Endgame_Enter
Char.SetObjEnterCarAsDriver($scplayer, $Sabre)
Char.SetObjEnterCarAsPassenger($EndOfGamePed, $Sabre)

:Endgame_CheckBoarding
while not Char.IsInCar($EndOfGamePed, $Sabre)
    wait 0
    if
        Char.IsInCar($EndOfGamePed, $Sabre)
    then
        if
            Char.IsInCar($scplayer, $Sabre)
        then
            goto @Endgame_Go
        end
    end

    if or
        Char.IsStuck($EndOfGamePed)
        Char.IsStuck($scplayer)
    then
        Char.SetCoordinates($EndOfGamePed, -530.692, -315.928, 13.62)
        Char.SetCoordinates($scplayer, -535.6, -325.521, 13.56)
        wait 500
        goto @Endgame_Enter
    end
end

goto @Endgame_CheckBoarding

:Endgame_Go
Camera.PointAtCar($Sabre, CameraMode.BehindCar, SwitchType.JumpCut)
Car.AddUpsidedownCheck($Sabre)
Car.GotoCoordinatesAccurate($Sabre, 240.4, -1280.2, 10.0)
Audio.PlayEndOfGameTune()

:Endgame_Stop
while not Car.IsStoppedInArea2D($Sabre, 235.4, -1275.2, 245.6, -1285.6, False)
    wait 0
    if
        Car.Locate2D($Sabre, 235.0, -1275.0, 10.0, 10.0, False)
    then
        Car.SetMission($Sabre, CarMission.None)
        goto @Endgame_Finish
    end
    if or
        Car.IsInWater($Sabre)
        Car.IsUpsidedown($Sabre)
    then
        goto @Endgame_ResetCutscene
    end
end

:Endgame_ResetCutscene
$ONMISSION = 0
Camera.DoFade(1500, Fade.Out)
wait 1500
if
    Char.DoesExist($EndOfGamePed)
then
    Char.Delete($EndOfGamePed)
end

if
    not Car.IsDead($Sabre)
then
    Car.Delete($Sabre)
end

Char.SetCoordinates($scplayer, -535.6, -325.521, 13.56)
goto @Endgame_LoadModels

:Endgame_Finish
Camera.DoFade(1500, Fade.Out)
wait 1500
Camera.PointAtChar($EndOfGamePed, CameraMode.FollowPed, SwitchType.JumpCut)
Audio.StopEndOfGameTune()
Char.SetObjLeaveCar($EndOfGamePed, $Sabre)
Char.SetObjLeaveCar($scplayer, $Sabre)
Stat.PlayerMadeProgress(20)
$Endgame = 1
$ONMISSION = 0

wait 1000
Camera.DoFade(1500, Fade.In)
wait 1500
Char.SetObjDestroyCar($EndOfGamePed, $Sabre)
Player.SetControl($PLAYER1, True)
Text.Print('Endgame', 5000, 0) // Go watch the mindless destruction!
Streaming.MarkModelAsNoLongerNeeded(#SABRE)
Streaming.MarkModelAsNoLongerNeeded(#WFYG1)
terminate_this_script

//-------------Mission 7---------------

:Fairy
$ONMISSION = 1
Streaming.RequestModel(#WFYBE)
Streaming.RequestModel(#WFYLG)
Streaming.RequestModel(#WFYSH)
Streaming.LoadAllModelsNow

:Fairy_LoadWait
wait 0
if
    Streaming.HasModelLoaded(#WFYBE)
then
    if
        Streaming.HasModelLoaded(#WFYLG)
    then
        if
            Streaming.HasModelLoaded(#WFYSH)
        then
            goto @Fairy_Start
        else
            goto @Fairy_LoadWait
        end
    else
        goto @Fairy_LoadWait
    end
else
    goto @Fairy_LoadWait
end

:Fairy_Start
$Fairy1 = Char.Create(PedType.CivFemale, #WFYBE, -371.9346, -1750.0928, 11.6376)
$Fairy2 = Char.Create(PedType.CivFemale, #WFYLG, -365.9346, -1750.0928, 11.6376)
$Fairy3 = Char.Create(PedType.CivFemale, #WFYSH, -360.9346, -1750.0928, 11.6376)
Char.TurnToFacePlayer($Fairy1, $PLAYER1)
Char.TurnToFacePlayer($Fairy2, $PLAYER1)
Char.TurnToFacePlayer($Fairy3, $PLAYER1)
Char.TurnToFaceChar($PLAYER1, $Fairy1)
Char.SetWaitState($Fairy1, WaitState.PlayAnimChat, 6789)
wait 500
Camera.DoFade(1500, Fade.In)
wait 1500
Text.PrintString("Greetings, child.", 3000)
wait 3000
Text.PrintString("We have been waiting so long for your arrival...", 5000)
wait 2000
Camera.DoFade(1500, Fade.Out)
wait 1500

if
    has_deatharrest_been_executed
then
    goto @Fairy_Fail
end

Char.RemoveElegantly($Fairy1)
Char.RemoveElegantly($Fairy2)
Char.RemoveElegantly($Fairy3)
$ONMISSION = 0
Streaming.MarkModelAsNoLongerNeeded(#WFYBE)
Streaming.MarkModelAsNoLongerNeeded(#WFYLG)
Streaming.MarkModelAsNoLongerNeeded(#WFYSH)
wait 3000
Camera.DoFade(1500, Fade.In)
wait 1000
Player.SetControl($PLAYER1, True)
terminate_this_script

:Fairy_Fail
Text.PrintBig('MISFAIL', 2000, TextStyle.Middle)
$ONMISSION = 0
if
    Char.DoesExist($Fairy1)
then
    Char.RemoveElegantly($Fairy1)
end
if
    Char.DoesExist($Fairy2)
then
    Char.RemoveElegantly($Fairy2)
end
if
    Char.DoesExist($Fairy3)
then
    Char.RemoveElegantly($Fairy3)
end
Streaming.MarkModelAsNoLongerNeeded(#WFYBE)
Streaming.MarkModelAsNoLongerNeeded(#WFYLG)
Streaming.MarkModelAsNoLongerNeeded(#WFYSH)
Player.SetControl($PLAYER1, True)
terminate_this_script
